Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the biological space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 911 and the convergence rate is O(681/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the biological space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 911 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 681. QED.

Theorem 218.4 (Invariance Principle)

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^518(S, F) is trivial. QED.
